Install and remove

A. apk

Dependencies come along, signatures checked:

apk add ros2-jazzy-control
apk add ros2-jazzy-control=20260128-r2   # this version

apk del ros2-jazzy-control   # also drops dependencies nothing else needs

Package: ros2-jazzy-control-20260128-r2.apk. Setting up apk: INSTALL.md 2A.

B-1. oss-get

No apk: target/oss-get downloads, checks the SHA-256, installs the dependencies first and keeps a file list per package:

oss-get update
oss-get install ros2-jazzy-control
oss-get install ros2-jazzy-control=20260128-r2   # this version

oss-get remove ros2-jazzy-control   # its files only

Its dependencies stay installed: console_bridge, curl, fastcdr, fmt, orocos-kdl, python3, python3-filelock, python3-lxml, python3-pyyaml, ros2, tinyxml2. Remove them too (oss-get remove <dep>) if nothing else needs them; oss-get refuses a package another installed one still needs.

B-2. By hand

Archive
https://github.com/openqnx/oss/releases/download/packages-8.0-aarch64-m-z/ros2-jazzy-control-20260128-r2.tar.gz (12.3 MiB)
SHA-256
44627ec91c4cdefec4820791723fc8fee9e95f15bc9bd2555ec141dcc2cf14c4
Install first
console_bridge, brotli, c-ares, nghttp2, openssl, zlib, zstd, curl, fastcdr, fmt, orocos-kdl, bzip2, expat, gdbm, libiconv, libunistring, ncurses, gettext, libffi, readline, sqlite, xz, python3, python3-filelock, libxml2, libxslt, python3-lxml, python3-pyyaml, benchmark, cyclonedds, foonathan_memory, tinyxml2, fastdds, libyaml, lz4, openblas, numpy, opencv, urdfdom, yaml-cpp, ros2 (in this order, each from its own page the same way; skip any already installed)

Install: download, check, save the file list, unpack under /data/oss:

mkdir -p /data/oss/dl /data/oss/var/lib/manual && cd /data/oss/dl
curl -fsSLO https://github.com/openqnx/oss/releases/download/packages-8.0-aarch64-m-z/ros2-jazzy-control-20260128-r2.tar.gz
[ "$(openssl dgst -sha256 ros2-jazzy-control-20260128-r2.tar.gz | sed 's/.*= *//')" = 44627ec91c4cdefec4820791723fc8fee9e95f15bc9bd2555ec141dcc2cf14c4 ] && echo OK || echo BAD
gzip -dc ros2-jazzy-control-20260128-r2.tar.gz | tar -tf - | grep -v '/$' > /data/oss/var/lib/manual/ros2-jazzy-control.list
gzip -dc ros2-jazzy-control-20260128-r2.tar.gz | (cd /data/oss && tar -xf -) && rm ros2-jazzy-control-20260128-r2.tar.gz

Remove: delete exactly the files it installed, then the list:

cd /data/oss && while read -r f; do rm -f "$f"; done < var/lib/manual/ros2-jazzy-control.list
rm var/lib/manual/ros2-jazzy-control.list

Its dependencies stay installed: console_bridge, brotli, c-ares, nghttp2, openssl, zlib, zstd, curl, fastcdr, fmt, orocos-kdl, bzip2, expat, gdbm, libiconv, libunistring, ncurses, gettext, libffi, readline, sqlite, xz, python3, python3-filelock, libxml2, libxslt, python3-lxml, python3-pyyaml, benchmark, cyclonedds, foonathan_memory, tinyxml2, fastdds, libyaml, lz4, openblas, numpy, opencv, urdfdom, yaml-cpp, ros2. Remove each the same way, from its own page, if nothing else needs it.

For a root shell on the target (QNX sh and the stock curl, openssl, gzip, tar; https needs SSL_CERT_FILE, see INSTALL.md). Empty directories stay. Don't remove a dependency another installed package still needs. Use one method per /data/oss: apk and oss-get do not know about files unpacked by hand.

Depends (18)

console_bridge, curl, fastcdr, fmt, orocos-kdl, python3, python3-filelock, python3-lxml, python3-pyyaml, ros2, tinyxml2, so:libconsole_bridge.so.1.0, so:libcurl.so, so:libfastcdr.so.2, so:libfmt.so.11, so:liborocos-kdl.so.1.5, so:libpython3.14.so.1.0, so:libtinyxml2.so.10

built against these libraries: apk installs only a version that provides them
